{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-19T191146Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/.claude/skills/frontend-playbook\n\n# Frontend Playbook\n\nFollow this workflow; do not treat a green build or successful upload as proof that the intended app shipped.\n\n## Start with the requested stack\n\nWhen the user wants Scaffold-ETH 2 or has not selected a stack for a conventional contract-plus-frontend dApp, use its generator:\n\n```bash\nnpx create-eth@2.0.23 # Tested version; update deliberately\n```\n\nUse the generated Foundry/Next.js monorepo, wallet integration, contract hooks, and components. Respect an explicitly requested alternative stack.\n\n## Choose the local chain deliberately\n\n- Use `yarn chain` for isolated contracts, mocks, and unit tests.\n- Use `yarn fork --network <chain>` when behavior depends on deployed protocols, tokens, balances, or other real chain state.\n- In fork mode, point the frontend at the local Anvil network (`chains.foundry`, chain ID 31337), not the upstream chain being copied. Switch to the real target chain only for a real deployment.\n\nA fork also grants powers the real chain does not. Fund demo identities from state that already exists rather than deploying a mock token:\n\n```bash\ncast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount <whale>\ncast send <token> \"transfer(address,uint256)\" <demo-account> <amount> \\\n  --from <whale> --unlocked\n```\n\n`anvil_setBalance` and `anvil_setStorageAt` are equivalent overrides when no suitable holder exists. Nothing here is broadcast; the fork is a local copy.\n\nAnvil normally mines only when a transaction arrives, so between transactions the latest block and `block.timestamp` remain frozen; the next transaction advances time in one jump. This silently breaks live deadlines, expiry, and vesting displays even when `vm.warp` unit tests pass. For continuous behavior, enable interval mining:\n\n```bash\ncast rpc anvil_setIntervalMining 1\n```\n\nFor a frozen live demo, enable interval mining. Mining a single block with `evm_mine` restamps `block.timestamp` once and it freezes again immediately; manual mining and `evm_increaseTime` are for controlled single-step tests, not a running demo. Add `--block-time 1` to the fork script when the project should always run that way.\n\n## Build a static IPFS release\n\nConfigure the IPFS build for static export, route directories, and static images:\n\n```typescript\nif (process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD === \"true\") {\n  nextConfig.output = \"export\";\n  nextConfig.trailingSlash = true;\n  nextConfig.images = { unoptimized: true };\n}\n```\n\nSet the production origin before building so Open Graph URLs and images do not resolve to localhost. Then remove old artifacts and rebuild:\n\n```bash\ncd packages/nextjs\nrm -rf .next out\nNEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL=\"https://<production-domain>\" \\\n  NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true \\\n  yarn build\n```\n\nOn Node 25, the built-in `localStorage` global can exist without the standard Web Storage methods when no backing file is configured; libraries detect it and crash on calls such as `getItem()` during static prerender. Apply a process-level remedy inherited by build workers, not only code in `instrumentation.ts` or `next.config.ts`. Depending on the app, use one of:\n\n```bash\nNODE_OPTIONS=\"--localstorage-file=.node-localstorage\"\nNODE_OPTIONS=\"--no-experimental-webstorage\"\n```\n\n## Verify before and after upload\n\nBefore uploading, confirm the expected change is in `out/`, that every route emitted its own directory, and that generated metadata carries the production origin:\n\n```bash\nls out/*/index.html # One directory with an index.html per route\n```\n\nAfter uploading:\n\n1. Record the CID. An unchanged CID means the uploaded bytes are identical; check the build output and upload target before blaming gateway caching.\n2. Load the root and at least one non-home route through the gateway. Root success does not prove exported routes resolve.\n\n   ```bash\n   curl -s -o /dev/null -w \"%{http_code}\" -L \"https://<gateway>/ipfs/<cid>/debug/\" # Expect 200, not 404\n   ```\n\n3. Verify the Open Graph image and URL use the production origin.\n4. For ENS, update the content hash only after the deployed CID has been reviewed and approved, then confirm the resolver and public gateway serve that CID.\n\nDo not claim the release is complete until these checks pass.\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f3abb02-a418-476d-94c8-4c1845e801a6","uuid":"7a399f22-5358-47e5-bc36-c6a22f310bff","timestamp":"2026-08-19T19:11:51.926Z","isSynthetic":true}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01PQuxqbRsignbwBXfK32yxe","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\n2\tpragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n3\t\n4\t// Useful for debugging. Remove when deploying to a live network.\n5\timport \"forge-std/console.sol\";\n6\t\n7\t// Use openzeppelin to inherit battle-tested implementations (ERC20, ERC721, etc)\n8\t// import \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\n9\t\n10\t/**\n11\t * A smart contract that allows changing a state variable of the contract and tracking the changes\n12\t * It also allows the owner to withdraw the Ether in the contract\n13\t * @author BuidlGuidl\n14\t */\n15\tcontract YourContract {\n16\t  // State Variables\n17\t  address public immutable owner;\n18\t  string public greeting = \"Building Unstoppable Apps!!!\";\n19\t  bool public premium = false;\n20\t  uint256 public totalCounter = 0;\n21\t  mapping(address => uint256) public userGreetingCounter;\n22\t\n23\t  // Events: a way to emit log statements from smart contract that can be listened to by external parties\n24\t  event GreetingChange(\n25\t    address indexed greetingSetter,\n26\t    string newGreeting,\n27\t    bool premium,\n28\t    uint256 value\n29\t  );\n30\t\n31\t  // Constructor: Called once on contract deployment\n32\t  // Check packages/foundry/deploy/Deploy.s.sol\n33\t  constructor(address _owner) {\n34\t    owner = _owner;\n35\t  }\n36\t\n37\t  // Modifier: used to define a set of rules that must be met before or after a function is executed\n38\t  // Check the withdraw() function\n39\t  modifier isOwner() {\n40\t    // msg.sender: predefined variable that represents address of the account that called the current function\n41\t    require(msg.sender == owner, \"Not the Owner\");\n42\t    _;\n43\t  }\n44\t\n45\t  /**\n46\t   * Function that allows anyone to change the state variable \"greeting\" of the contract and increase the counters\n47\t   *\n48\t   * @param _newGreeting (string memory) - new greeting to save on the contract\n49\t   */\n50\t  function setGreeting(string memory _newGreeting) public payable {\n51\t    // Print data to the anvil chain console. Remove when deploying to a live network.\n52\t\n53\t    console.logString(\"Setting new greeting\");\n54\t    console.logString(_newGreeting);\n55\t\n56\t    greeting = _newGreeting;\n57\t    totalCounter += 1;\n58\t    userGreetingCounter[msg.sender] += 1;\n59\t\n60\t    // msg.value: built-in global variable that represents the amount of ether sent with the transaction\n61\t    if (msg.value > 0) {\n62\t      premium = true;\n63\t    } else {\n64\t      premium = false;\n65\t    }\n66\t\n67\t    // emit: keyword used to trigger an event\n68\t    emit GreetingChange(msg.sender, _newGreeting, msg.value > 0, msg.value);\n69\t  }\n70\t\n71\t  /**\n72\t   * Function that allows the owner to withdraw all the Ether in the contract\n73\t   * The function can only be called by the owner of the contract as defined by the isOwner modifier\n74\t   */\n75\t  function withdraw() public isOwner {\n76\t    (bool success,) = owner.call{ value: address(this).balance }(\"\");\n77\t    require(success, \"Failed to send Ether\");\n78\t  }\n79\t\n80\t  /**\n81\t   * Function that allows the contract to receive ETH\n82\t   */\n83\t  receive() external payable { }\n84\t}\n85\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f3abb02-a418-476d-94c8-4c1845e801a6","uuid":"17e7457e-8f6d-4f8b-8d71-6260ae05d25f","timestamp":"2026-08-19T19:14:40.625Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-19T191146Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/foundry/contracts/YourContract.sol","content":"//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\n// Useful for debugging. Remove when deploying to a live network.\nimport \"forge-std/console.sol\";\n\n// Use openzeppelin to inherit battle-tested implementations (ERC20, ERC721, etc)\n// import \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\n\n/**\n * A smart contract that allows changing a state variable of the contract and tracking the changes\n * It also allows the owner to withdraw the Ether in the contract\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract YourContract {\n  // State Variables\n  address public immutable owner;\n  string public greeting = \"Building Unstoppable Apps!!!\";\n  bool public premium = false;\n  uint256 public totalCounter = 0;\n  mapping(address => uint256) public userGreetingCounter;\n\n  // Events: a way to emit log statements from smart contract that can be listened to by external parties\n  event GreetingChange(\n    address indexed greetingSetter,\n    string newGreeting,\n    bool premium,\n    uint256 value\n  );\n\n  // Constructor: Called once on contract deployment\n  // Check packages/foundry/deploy/Deploy.s.sol\n  constructor(address _owner) {\n    owner = _owner;\n  }\n\n  // Modifier: used to define a set of rules that must be met before or after a function is executed\n  // Check the withdraw() function\n  modifier isOwner() {\n    // msg.sender: predefined variable that represents address of the account that called the current function\n    require(msg.sender == owner, \"Not the Owner\");\n    _;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows anyone to change the state variable \"greeting\" of the contract and increase the counters\n   *\n   * @param _newGreeting (string memory) - new greeting to save on the contract\n   */\n  function setGreeting(string memory _newGreeting) public payable {\n    // Print data to the anvil chain console. Remove when deploying to a live network.\n\n    console.logString(\"Setting new greeting\");\n    console.logString(_newGreeting);\n\n    greeting = _newGreeting;\n    totalCounter += 1;\n    userGreetingCounter[msg.sender] += 1;\n\n    // msg.value: built-in global variable that represents the amount of ether sent with the transaction\n    if (msg.value > 0) {\n      premium = true;\n    } else {\n      premium = false;\n    }\n\n    // emit: keyword used to trigger an event\n    emit GreetingChange(msg.sender, _newGreeting, msg.value > 0, msg.value);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows the owner to withdraw all the Ether in the contract\n   * The function can only be called by the owner of the contract as defined by the isOwner modifier\n   */\n  function withdraw() public isOwner {\n    (bool success,) = owner.call{ value: address(this).balance }(\"\");\n    require(success, \"Failed to send Ether\");\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows the contract to receive ETH\n   */\n  receive() external payable { }\n}\n","numLines":85,"startLine":1,"totalLines":85}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCeCk792PwPzt12aqPkm","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Hp9TURXBwCVVbd7SjbwvMQ","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-19T191146Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol","content":"//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\n/// Minimal ERC20 surface the tip jar needs from USDC.\ninterface IERC20 {\n  function transferFrom(address from, address to, uint256 amount) external returns (bool);\n  function transfer(address to, uint256 amount) external returns (bool);\n  function balanceOf(address account) external view returns (uint256);\n}\n\n/**\n * A tip jar that accepts USDC tips and keeps an onchain feed of them.\n *\n * Tippers approve the jar to spend their USDC, then call `tip` with an amount\n * and an optional message. Every tip is stored and emitted so the frontend can\n * render a live feed. The owner can withdraw the collected USDC.\n *\n * USDC has 6 decimals, so an `amount` of 1_000_000 equals 1 USDC.\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract TipJar {\n  // The USDC token this jar collects (Base USDC in production, a fork copy locally).\n  IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n  // The address allowed to withdraw collected tips.\n  address public immutable owner;\n\n  struct Tip {\n    address tipper;\n    uint256 amount;\n    string message;\n    uint256 timestamp;\n  }\n\n  // Full history of tips, oldest first. Powers the feed.\n  Tip[] public tips;\n  // Running total of all USDC tipped (in USDC's 6-decimal units).\n  uint256 public totalTipped;\n  // How much each address has tipped in total.\n  mapping(address => uint256) public tippedByAddress;\n\n  event NewTip(address indexed tipper, uint256 amount, string message, uint256 timestamp);\n  event Withdrawal(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n\n  constructor(address _usdc, address _owner) {\n    require(_usdc != address(0), \"USDC address required\");\n    usdc = IERC20(_usdc);\n    owner = _owner;\n  }\n\n  modifier onlyOwner() {\n    require(msg.sender == owner, \"Not the owner\");\n    _;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Send a USDC tip with an optional message. The caller must have approved\n   * this contract to spend at least `amount` of their USDC first.\n   *\n   * @param amount USDC amount in 6-decimal units (1 USDC == 1_000_000)\n   * @param message optional note shown in the feed (max 280 bytes)\n   */\n  function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n    require(amount > 0, \"Tip must be greater than 0\");\n    require(bytes(message).length <= 280, \"Message too long\");\n\n    bool success = usdc.transferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n    require(success, \"USDC transfer failed\");\n\n    tips.push(Tip({ tipper: msg.sender, amount: amount, message: message, timestamp: block.timestamp }));\n    totalTipped += amount;\n    tippedByAddress[msg.sender] += amount;\n\n    emit NewTip(msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp);\n  }\n\n  /// The whole tip feed in one call, for easy frontend reads.\n  function getTips() external view returns (Tip[] memory) {\n    return tips;\n  }\n\n  /// Number of tips received.\n  function tipCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n    return tips.length;\n  }\n\n  /// Current USDC balance held by the jar.\n  function jarBalance() external view returns (uint256) {\n    return usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n  }\n\n  /// Owner sweeps the collected USDC to their address.\n  function withdraw() external onlyOwner {\n    uint256 bal = usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n    require(bal > 0, \"Nothing to withdraw\");\n    require(usdc.transfer(owner, bal), \"Withdraw failed\");\n    emit Withdrawal(owner, bal);\n  }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":227,"cache_read_input_tokens":29684,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":227},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f3abb02-a418-476d-94c8-4c1845e801a6","uuid":"70d9d582-e472-40f9-bd73-7378510973f0","timestamp":"2026-08-19T19:15:52.033Z","request_id":"req_011CeCeCj8rAnoXg1Q4f79GN"}

Every tip is stored and emitted so the frontend can\n * render a live feed. The owner can withdraw the collected USDC.\n *\n * USDC has 6 decimals, so an `amount` of 1_000_000 equals 1 USDC.\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract TipJar {\n  // The USDC token this jar collects (Base USDC in production, a fork copy locally).\n  IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n  // The address allowed to withdraw collected tips.\n  address public immutable owner;\n\n  struct Tip {\n    address tipper;\n    uint256 amount;\n    string message;\n    uint256 timestamp;\n  }\n\n  // Full history of tips, oldest first. Powers the feed.\n  Tip[] public tips;\n  // Running total of all USDC tipped (in USDC's 6-decimal units).\n  uint256 public totalTipped;\n  // How much each address has tipped in total.\n  mapping(address => uint256) public tippedByAddress;\n\n  event NewTip(address indexed tipper, uint256 amount, string message, uint256 timestamp);\n  event Withdrawal(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n\n  constructor(address _usdc, address _owner) {\n    require(_usdc != address(0), \"USDC address required\");\n    usdc = IERC20(_usdc);\n    owner = _owner;\n  }\n\n  modifier onlyOwner() {\n    require(msg.sender == owner, \"Not the owner\");\n    _;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Send a USDC tip with an optional message. The caller must have approved\n   * this contract to spend at least `amount` of their USDC first.\n   *\n   * @param amount USDC amount in 6-decimal units (1 USDC == 1_000_000)\n   * @param message optional note shown in the feed (max 280 bytes)\n   */\n  function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n    require(amount > 0, \"Tip must be greater than 0\");\n    require(bytes(message).length <= 280, \"Message too long\");\n\n    bool success = usdc.transferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n    require(success, \"USDC transfer failed\");\n\n    tips.push(Tip({ tipper: msg.sender, amount: amount, message: message, timestamp: block.timestamp }));\n    totalTipped += amount;\n    tippedByAddress[msg.sender] += amount;\n\n    emit NewTip(msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp);\n  }\n\n  /// The whole tip feed in one call, for easy frontend reads.\n  function getTips() external view returns (Tip[] memory) {\n    return tips;\n  }\n\n  /// Number of tips received.\n  function tipCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n    return tips.length;\n  }\n\n  /// Current USDC balance held by the jar.\n  function jarBalance() external view returns (uint256) {\n    return usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n  }\n\n  /// Owner sweeps the collected USDC to their address.\n  function withdraw() external onlyOwner {\n    uint256 bal = usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n    require(bal > 0, \"Nothing to withdraw\");\n    require(usdc.transfer(owner, bal), \"Withdraw failed\");\n    emit Withdrawal(owner, bal);\n  }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01TTmKCFYGWU5zzPweseqyRc","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\n2\tpragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n3\t\n4\timport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\n5\timport { DeployYourContract } from \"./DeployYourContract.s.sol\";\n6\t\n7\t/**\n8\t * @notice Main deployment script for all contracts\n9\t * @dev Run this when you want to deploy multiple contracts at once\n10\t *\n11\t * Example: yarn deploy # {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015hNj3um7TkQA2hZkXCrr7Y","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\n2\tpragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n3\t\n4\timport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\n5\timport \"../contracts/YourContract.sol\";\n6\t\n7\t/**\n8\t * @notice Deploy script for YourContract contract\n9\t * @dev Inherits ScaffoldETHDeploy which:\n10\t *      - Includes forge-std/Script.sol for deployment\n11\t *      - Includes ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner modifier\n12\t *      - Provides `deployer` variable\n13\t * Example:\n14\t * yarn deploy --file DeployYourContract.s.sol  # local anvil chain\n15\t * yarn deploy --file DeployYourContract.s.sol --network optimism # live network (requires keystore)\n16\t */\n17\tcontract DeployYourContract is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n18\t    /**\n19\t     * @dev Deployer setup based on `ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT` in `.env`:\n20\t     *      - \"scaffold-eth-default\": Uses Anvil's account #9 (0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720), no password prompt\n21\t     *      - \"scaffold-eth-custom\": requires password used while creating keystore\n22\t     *\n23\t     * Note: Must use ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner modifier to:\n24\t     *      - Setup correct `deployer` account and fund it\n25\t     *      - Export contract addresses & ABIs to `nextjs` packages\n26\t     */\n27\t    function run() external ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner {\n28\t        new YourContract(deployer);\n29\t    }\n30\t}\n31\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f3abb02-a418-476d-94c8-4c1845e801a6","uuid":"2821dabf-e03f-45ae-869c-2d8014ef475a","timestamp":"2026-08-19T19:15:57.772Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-19T191146Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/foundry/script/DeployYourContract.s.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\nimport \"../contracts/YourContract.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice Deploy script for YourContract contract\n * @dev Inherits ScaffoldETHDeploy which:\n *      - Includes forge-std/Script.sol for deployment\n *      - Includes ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner modifier\n *      - Provides `deployer` variable\n * Example:\n * yarn deploy --file DeployYourContract.s.sol  # local anvil chain\n * yarn deploy --file DeployYourContract.s.sol --network optimism # live network (requires keystore)\n */\ncontract DeployYourContract is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n    /**\n     * @dev Deployer setup based on `ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT` in `.env`:\n     *      - \"scaffold-eth-default\": Uses Anvil's account #9 (0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720), no password prompt\n     *      - \"scaffold-eth-custom\": requires password used while creating keystore\n     *\n     * Note: Must use ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner modifier to:\n     *      - Setup correct `deployer` account and fund it\n     *      - Export contract addresses & ABIs to `nextjs` packages\n     */\n    function run() external ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner {\n        new YourContract(deployer);\n    }\n}\n","numLines":31,"startLine":1,"totalLines":31}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WgEQYbzw7TeWqzDyWAL85U","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t// SPDX-License-Identifier: UNLICENSED\n2\tpragma solidity ^0.8.13;\n3\t\n4\timport \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\n5\timport \"../contracts/YourContract.sol\";\n6\t\n7\tcontract YourContractTest is Test {\n8\t  YourContract public yourContract;\n9\t\n10\t  function setUp() public {\n11\t    yourContract = new YourContract(vm.addr(1));\n12\t  }\n13\t\n14\t  function testMessageOnDeployment() public view {\n15\t    require(\n16\t      keccak256(bytes(yourContract.greeting()))\n17\t        == keccak256(\"Building Unstoppable Apps!!!\")\n18\t    );\n19\t  }\n20\t}\n21\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f3abb02-a418-476d-94c8-4c1845e801a6","uuid":"7958b722-ce1f-42ef-af6c-218685bddb05","timestamp":"2026-08-19T19:16:24.272Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-19T191146Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/foundry/test/YourContract.t.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: UNLICENSED\npragma solidity ^0.8.13;\n\nimport \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\nimport \"../contracts/YourContract.sol\";\n\ncontract YourContractTest is Test {\n  YourContract public yourContract;\n\n  function setUp() public {\n    yourContract = new YourContract(vm.addr(1));\n  }\n\n  function testMessageOnDeployment() public view {\n    require(\n      keccak256(bytes(yourContract.greeting()))\n        == keccak256(\"Building Unstoppable Apps!!!\")\n    );\n  }\n}\n","numLines":21,"startLine":1,"totalLines":21}}}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCeHPjgUJWbb43i6vBrV","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Ge5hhsduRXnvHXukPPzsVH","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-19T191146Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: UNLICENSED\npragma solidity ^0.8.13;\n\nimport \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\nimport \"../contracts/TipJar.sol\";\n\n/// A tiny 6-decimal ERC20 that mimics USDC for isolated unit tests.\ncontract MockUSDC is IERC20 {\n  mapping(address => uint256) public balanceOf;\n  mapping(address => mapping(address => uint256)) public allowance;\n\n  function mint(address to, uint256 amount) external {\n    balanceOf[to] += amount;\n  }\n\n  function approve(address spender, uint256 amount) external returns (bool) {\n    allowance[msg.sender][spender] = amount;\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  function transfer(address to, uint256 amount) external returns (bool) {\n    balanceOf[msg.sender] -= amount;\n    balanceOf[to] += amount;\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  function transferFrom(address from, address to, uint256 amount) external returns (bool) {\n    allowance[from][msg.sender] -= amount;\n    balanceOf[from] -= amount;\n    balanceOf[to] += amount;\n    return true;\n  }\n}\n\ncontract TipJarTest is Test {\n  MockUSDC public usdc;\n  TipJar public jar;\n  address owner = vm.addr(1);\n  address alice = vm.addr(2);\n\n  function setUp() public {\n    usdc = new MockUSDC();\n    jar = new TipJar(address(usdc), owner);\n    usdc.mint(alice, 100_000_000); // 100 USDC\n  }\n\n  function testTipRecordsFeedAndTotals() public {\n    vm.startPrank(alice);\n    usdc.approve(address(jar), 5_000_000);\n    jar.tip(5_000_000, \"thanks!\");\n    vm.stopPrank();\n\n    assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 1);\n    assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 5_000_000);\n    assertEq(jar.tippedByAddress(alice), 5_000_000);\n    assertEq(jar.jarBalance(), 5_000_000);\n\n    TipJar.Tip[] memory feed = jar.getTips();\n    assertEq(feed[0].tipper, alice);\n    assertEq(feed[0].amount, 5_000_000);\n    assertEq(feed[0].message, \"thanks!\");\n  }\n\n  function testTipRejectsZero() public {\n    vm.prank(alice);\n    vm.expectRevert(\"Tip must be greater than 0\");\n    jar.tip(0, \"\");\n  }\n\n  function testOnlyOwnerWithdraws() public {\n    vm.startPrank(alice);\n    usdc.approve(address(jar), 5_000_000);\n    jar.tip(5_000_000, \"gm\");\n    vm.stopPrank();\n\n    vm.prank(alice);\n    vm.expectRevert(\"Not the owner\");\n    jar.withdraw();\n\n    vm.prank(owner);\n    jar.withdraw();\n    assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), 5_000_000);\n    assertEq(jar.jarBalance(), 0);\n  }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":499,"cache_read_input_tokens":36212,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":499},"output_tokens":21,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f3abb02-a418-476d-94c8-4c1845e801a6","uuid":"4781bf28-6e81-4c0b-932b-b8dc9340cbfe","timestamp":"2026-08-19T19:16:40.575Z","request_id":"req_011CeCeHNvpBEESg6LANXZK5"}

vm.stopPrank();\n\n    vm.prank(alice);\n    vm.expectRevert(\"Not the owner\");\n    jar.withdraw();\n\n    vm.prank(owner);\n    jar.withdraw();\n    assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), 5_000_000);\n    assertEq(jar.jarBalance(), 0);\n  }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCeXSp7Nwf8FAZHLdZ3R","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Slot 9 works for USDC balances. Let me create a reusable funding script and a feed-seeding script for the demo."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":449,"cache_read_input_tokens":52549,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":449},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f3abb02-a418-476d-94c8-4c1845e801a6","uuid":"09ca071f-e99f-4bc3-8fe2-8526b6ccba1d","timestamp":"2026-08-19T19:19:46.327Z","request_id":"req_011CeCeXRTkTay5nX1bTrBmv"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCeXSp7Nwf8FAZHLdZ3R","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01BKGsKMmQEYUA23jG8mVveA","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-19T191146Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/scripts/fund-usdc.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Give a local demo address USDC (and some ETH for gas) on the running Base fork.\n#\n# Usage: ./scripts/fund-usdc.sh <address> [usdc_amount]\n#   usdc_amount is in whole USDC (default 1000). USDC has 6 decimals.\n#\n# Works by writing directly to the forked USDC contract's balance storage slot,\n# which is safe because the fork is a throwaway local copy of Base.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nADDR=\"${1:?Usage: fund-usdc.sh <address> [usdc_amount]}\"\nWHOLE_USDC=\"${2:-1000}\"\nRPC=\"${RPC_URL:-http://127.0.0.1:8545}\"\nUSDC=\"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\"\n\n# USDC (FiatTokenV2_2) keeps balances in the mapping at storage slot 9.\nSLOT=$(cast index address \"$ADDR\" 9)\nAMOUNT=$((WHOLE_USDC * 1000000))\nAMOUNT_HEX=$(cast to-uint256 \"$AMOUNT\")\n\ncast rpc anvil_setStorageAt \"$USDC\" \"$SLOT\" \"$AMOUNT_HEX\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\n# Top up ETH for gas (10 ETH).\ncast rpc anvil_setBalance \"$ADDR\" 0x8ac7230489e80000 --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\n\necho \"Funded $ADDR:\"\necho \"  USDC: $(cast call \"$USDC\" 'balanceOf(address)(uint256)' \"$ADDR\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\")\"\necho \"  ETH:  $(cast balance \"$ADDR\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\") wei\"\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":449,"cache_read_input_tokens":52549,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":449},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f3abb02-a418-476d-94c8-4c1845e801a6","uuid":"cefa57a7-6602-4642-b35e-d21707211e8a","timestamp":"2026-08-19T19:19:54.904Z","request_id":"req_011CeCeXRTkTay5nX1bTrBmv"}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01BKGsKMmQEYUA23jG8mVveA","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-19T191146Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/scripts/fund-usdc.sh (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f3abb02-a418-476d-94c8-4c1845e801a6","uuid":"e6c1f5a9-af54-4890-92f7-b45dd20a9e14","timestamp":"2026-08-19T19:19:54.912Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-19T191146Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/scripts/fund-usdc.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Give a local demo address USDC (and some ETH for gas) on the running Base fork.\n#\n# Usage: ./scripts/fund-usdc.sh <address> [usdc_amount]\n#   usdc_amount is in whole USDC (default 1000). USDC has 6 decimals.\n#\n# Works by writing directly to the forked USDC contract's balance storage slot,\n# which is safe because the fork is a throwaway local copy of Base.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nADDR=\"${1:?Usage: fund-usdc.sh <address> [usdc_amount]}\"\nWHOLE_USDC=\"${2:-1000}\"\nRPC=\"${RPC_URL:-http://127.0.0.1:8545}\"\nUSDC=\"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\"\n\n# USDC (FiatTokenV2_2) keeps balances in the mapping at storage slot 9.\nSLOT=$(cast index address \"$ADDR\" 9)\nAMOUNT=$((WHOLE_USDC * 1000000))\nAMOUNT_HEX=$(cast to-uint256 \"$AMOUNT\")\n\ncast rpc anvil_setStorageAt \"$USDC\" \"$SLOT\" \"$AMOUNT_HEX\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\n# Top up ETH for gas (10 ETH).\ncast rpc anvil_setBalance \"$ADDR\" 0x8ac7230489e80000 --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\n\necho \"Funded $ADDR:\"\necho \"  USDC: $(cast call \"$USDC\" 'balanceOf(address)(uint256)' \"$ADDR\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\")\"\necho \"  ETH:  $(cast balance \"$ADDR\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\") wei\"\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCeYxJRX1f4wq6p68mQE","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01TFN3cqfpGGRHuZSdqeiz7r","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-19T191146Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/scripts/seed-tips.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Populate the tip feed with a few sample tips so the page isn't empty on first load.\n# Uses Anvil's well-known demo accounts (public test keys, local only).\n#\n# Usage: ./scripts/seed-tips.sh [tipjar_address]\n# If no address is given, it's read from packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nRPC=\"${RPC_URL:-http://127.0.0.1:8545}\"\nUSDC=\"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\"\nHERE=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\n\nTIPJAR=\"${1:-}\"\nif [ -z \"$TIPJAR\" ]; then\n  TIPJAR=$(grep -oE 'address: \"0x[a-fA-F0-9]{40}\"' \"$HERE/../packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts\" | head -1 | grep -oE '0x[a-fA-F0-9]{40}')\nfi\necho \"Seeding tips into TipJar at $TIPJAR\"\n\n# Anvil default accounts (public, deterministic test keys).\nKEYS=(\n  \"0x59c6995e998f97a5a0044966f0945389dc9e86dae88c7a8412f4603b6b78690d\"\n  \"0x5de4111afa1a4b94908f83103eb1f1706367c2e68ca870fc3fb9a804cdab365a\"\n  \"0x7c852118294e51e653712a81e05800f419141751be58f605c371e15141b007a6\"\n)\nAMOUNTS=(5000000 2500000 10000000)          # 5, 2.5, 10 USDC\nMESSAGES=(\"Love this project! ☕\" \"Keep building 🚀\" \"Coffee's on me\")\n\nfor i in \"${!KEYS[@]}\"; do\n  PK=\"${KEYS[$i]}\"\n  ADDR=$(cast wallet address \"$PK\")\n  \"$HERE/fund-usdc.sh\" \"$ADDR\" 1000 >/dev/null\n  cast send \"$USDC\" \"approve(address,uint256)\" \"$TIPJAR\" \"${AMOUNTS[$i]}\" \\\n    --private-key \"$PK\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\n  cast send \"$TIPJAR\" \"tip(uint256,string)\" \"${AMOUNTS[$i]}\" \"${MESSAGES[$i]}\" \\\n    --private-key \"$PK\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\n  echo \"  tip from $ADDR: ${MESSAGES[$i]}\"\ndone\n\necho \"Total tips now: $(cast call \"$TIPJAR\" 'tipCount()(uint256)' --rpc-url \"$RPC\")\"\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1546,"cache_read_input_tokens":52998,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1546},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f3abb02-a418-476d-94c8-4c1845e801a6","uuid":"65198eba-3a48-4031-8a3f-29e34684dedd","timestamp":"2026-08-19T19:20:25.652Z","request_id":"req_011CeCeYvmtm1v5EoXiiBhhz"}

☕\" \"Keep building 🚀\" \"Coffee's on me\")\n\nfor i in \"${!KEYS[@]}\"; do\n  PK=\"${KEYS[$i]}\"\n  ADDR=$(cast wallet address \"$PK\")\n  \"$HERE/fund-usdc.sh\" \"$ADDR\" 1000 >/dev/null\n  cast send \"$USDC\" \"approve(address,uint256)\" \"$TIPJAR\" \"${AMOUNTS[$i]}\" \\\n    --private-key \"$PK\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\n  cast send \"$TIPJAR\" \"tip(uint256,string)\" \"${AMOUNTS[$i]}\" \"${MESSAGES[$i]}\" \\\n    --private-key \"$PK\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\n  echo \"  tip from $ADDR: ${MESSAGES[$i]}\"\ndone\n\necho \"Total tips now: $(cast call \"$TIPJAR\" 'tipCount()(uint256)' --rpc-url \"$RPC\")\"\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JD4UMzcqnBYQKovLPMZHKL","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\n2\t\"use client\";\n3\t\n4\timport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\n5\timport { Address } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\n6\timport type { NextPage } from \"next\";\n7\timport Link from \"next/link\";\n8\timport { BugAntIcon, MagnifyingGlassIcon } from {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCei5WWW5nwdmYEQcAc3","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Xhz1AdnKwapNrR5QXp4HzN","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-19T191146Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/nextjs/components/tipjar/TipForm.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { USDC_DECIMALS, formatUsdc } from \"~~/utils/tipjar\";\nimport {\n  useDeployedContractInfo,\n  useScaffoldReadContract,\n  useScaffoldWriteContract,\n} from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst PRESETS = [\"1\", \"5\", \"10\"];\n\nexport const TipForm = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress } = useAccount();\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [message, setMessage] = useState(\"\");\n  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);\n\n  const { data: tipJar } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n    watch: true,\n  });\n  const { data: allowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, tipJar?.address],\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeUsdc } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeTipJar } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n\n  const parsedAmount = (() => {\n    try {\n      return amount ? parseUnits(amount, USDC_DECIMALS) : 0n;\n    } catch {\n      return 0n;\n    }\n  })();\n\n  const insufficientBalance = usdcBalance !== undefined && parsedAmount > usdcBalance;\n\n  const handleTip = async () => {\n    if (!connectedAddress) {\n      notification.error(\"Connect your wallet first\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (parsedAmount <= 0n) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a tip amount greater than 0\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (insufficientBalance) {\n      notification.error(\"Not enough USDC in your wallet\");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    try {\n      setSubmitting(true);\n      // USDC must be approved for the jar before it can pull the tip.\n      if (allowance === undefined || allowance < parsedAmount) {\n        await writeUsdc({\n          functionName: \"approve\",\n          args: [tipJar?.address, parsedAmount],\n        });\n      }\n      await writeTipJar({\n        functionName: \"tip\",\n        args: [parsedAmount, message],\n      });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMessage(\"\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      // Errors are surfaced by the transactor's own notifications.\n      console.error(e);\n    } finally {\n      setSubmitting(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl\">\n      <div className=\"card-body\">\n        <h2 className=\"card-title\">Send a tip</h2>\n\n        <label className=\"label\">\n          <span className=\"label-text\">Amount (USDC)</span>\n          {connectedAddress && (\n            <span className=\"label-text-alt\">Balance: ${formatUsdc(usdcBalance)}</span>\n          )}\n        </label>\n        <input\n          type=\"number\"\n          min=\"0\"\n          step=\"0.01\"\n          placeholder=\"0.00\"\n          className=\"input input-bordered w-full\"\n          value={amount}\n          onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n        />\n        <div className=\"flex gap-2 mt-2\">\n          {PRESETS.map(p => (\n            <button key={p} type=\"button\" className=\"btn btn-sm btn-outline\" onClick={() => setAmount(p)}>\n              ${p}\n            </button>\n          ))}\n        </div>\n\n        <label className=\"label mt-2\">\n          <span className=\"label-text\">Message (optional)</span>\n          <span className=\"label-text-alt\">{message.length}/280</span>\n        </label>\n        <textarea\n          className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full\"\n          placeholder=\"Say something nice…\"\n          maxLength={280}\n          value={message}\n          onChange={e => setMessage(e.target.value)}\n        />\n\n        <button\n          className=\"btn btn-primary mt-4\"\n          disabled={!connectedAddress || submitting || parsedAmount <= 0n || insufficientBalance}\n          onClick={handleTip}\n        >\n          {submitting ? (\n            <>\n              <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\"></span> Sending…\n            </>\n          ) : (\n            \"Send tip ☕\"\n          )}\n        </button>\n        {!connectedAddress && (\n          <p className=\"text-center text-sm text-base-content/60 mt-1\">\n            Connect your wallet to send a tip.\n          </p>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":551,"cache_read_input_tokens":65135,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":551},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f3abb02-a418-476d-94c8-4c1845e801a6","uuid":"867493b9-3585-4fa6-9697-8395da5dd4ec","timestamp":"2026-08-19T19:22:23.586Z","request_id":"req_011CeCei4ZCvo9sS2iwhoXmp"}

client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { USDC_DECIMALS, formatUsdc } from \"~~/utils/tipjar\";\nimport {\n  useDeployedContractInfo,\n  useScaffoldReadContract,\n  useScaffoldWriteContract,\n} from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst PRESETS = [\"1\", \"5\", \"10\"];\n\nexport const TipForm = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress } = useAccount();\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [message, setMessage] = useState(\"\");\n  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);\n\n  const { data: tipJar } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n    watch: true,\n  });\n  const { data: allowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, tipJar?.address],\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeUsdc } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeTipJar } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n\n  const parsedAmount = (() => {\n    try {\n      return amount ? parseUnits(amount, USDC_DECIMALS) : 0n;\n    } catch {\n      return 0n;\n    }\n  })();\n\n  const insufficientBalance = usdcBalance !== undefined && parsedAmount > usdcBalance;\n\n  const handleTip = async () => {\n    if (!connectedAddress) {\n      notification.error(\"Connect your wallet first\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (parsedAmount <= 0n) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a tip amount greater than 0\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (insufficientBalance) {\n      notification.error(\"Not enough USDC in your wallet\");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    try {\n      setSubmitting(true);\n      // USDC must be approved for the jar before it can pull the tip.\n      if (allowance === undefined || allowance < parsedAmount) {\n        await writeUsdc({\n          functionName: \"approve\",\n          args: [tipJar?.address, parsedAmount],\n        });\n      }\n      await writeTipJar({\n        functionName: \"tip\",\n        args: [parsedAmount, message],\n      });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMessage(\"\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      // Errors are surfaced by the transactor's own notifications.\n      console.error(e);\n    } finally {\n      setSubmitting(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl\">\n      <div className=\"card-body\">\n        <h2 className=\"card-title\">Send a tip</h2>\n\n        <label className=\"label\">\n          <span className=\"label-text\">Amount (USDC)</span>\n          {connectedAddress && (\n            <span className=\"label-text-alt\">Balance: ${formatUsdc(usdcBalance)}</span>\n          )}\n        </label>\n        <input\n          type=\"number\"\n          min=\"0\"\n          step=\"0.01\"\n          placeholder=\"0.00\"\n          className=\"input input-bordered w-full\"\n          value={amount}\n          onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n        />\n        <div className=\"flex gap-2 mt-2\">\n          {PRESETS.map(p => (\n            <button key={p} type=\"button\" className=\"btn btn-sm btn-outline\" onClick={() => setAmount(p)}>\n              ${p}\n            </button>\n          ))}\n        </div>\n\n        <label className=\"label mt-2\">\n          <span className=\"label-text\">Message (optional)</span>\n          <span className=\"label-text-alt\">{message.length}/280</span>\n        </label>\n        <textarea\n          className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full\"\n          placeholder=\"Say something nice…\"\n          maxLength={280}\n          value={message}\n          onChange={e => setMessage(e.target.value)}\n        />\n\n        <button\n          className=\"btn btn-primary mt-4\"\n          disabled={!connectedAddress || submitting || parsedAmount <= 0n || insufficientBalance}\n          onClick={handleTip}\n        >\n          {submitting ? (\n            <>\n              <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\"></span> Sending…\n            </>\n          ) : (\n            \"Send tip ☕\"\n          )}\n        </button>\n        {!connectedAddress && (\n          <p className=\"text-center text-sm text-base-content/60 mt-1\">\n            Connect your wallet to send a tip.\n          </p>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCejtK7tb8NPNxMgnpLX","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01D9zDpjqWauPuQJkvMypXVu","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-19T191146Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/nextjs/components/tipjar/TipFeed.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { Address } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\nimport { formatUsdc } from \"~~/utils/tipjar\";\nimport { useScaffoldReadContract } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst timeAgo = (timestamp: bigint): string => {\n  const seconds = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) - Number(timestamp);\n  if (seconds < 60) return \"just now\";\n  const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);\n  if (minutes < 60) return `${minutes}m ago`;\n  const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);\n  if (hours < 24) return `${hours}h ago`;\n  return `${Math.floor(hours / 24)}d ago`;\n};\n\nexport const TipFeed = () => {\n  const { data: tips, isLoading } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"TipJar\",\n    functionName: \"getTips\",\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  // Newest first.\n  const feed = tips ? [...tips].reverse() : [];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl\">\n      <div className=\"card-body\">\n        <h2 className=\"card-title\">Recent tips</h2>\n\n        {isLoading && <p className=\"text-base-content/60\">Loading feed…</p>}\n        {!isLoading && feed.length === 0 && (\n          <p className=\"text-base-content/60\">No tips yet. Be the first to drop one in the jar!</p>\n        )}\n\n        <ul className=\"flex flex-col gap-3 max-h-[28rem] overflow-y-auto\">\n          {feed.map((tip, i) => (\n            <li key={`${tip.timestamp}-${i}`} className=\"border border-base-300 rounded-box p-3\">\n              <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-2\">\n                <Address address={tip.tipper} size=\"sm\" />\n                <span className=\"badge badge-primary badge-lg font-semibold\">${formatUsdc(tip.amount)}</span>\n              </div>\n              {tip.message && <p className=\"mt-2 break-words\">{tip.message}</p>}\n              <p className=\"text-xs text-base-content/50 mt-1\">{timeAgo(tip.timestamp)}</p>\n            </li>\n          ))}\n        </ul>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2804,"cache_read_input_tokens":65686,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2804},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f3abb02-a418-476d-94c8-4c1845e801a6","uuid":"0e9ce75e-2001-441d-b53f-3bb82334069f","timestamp":"2026-08-19T19:22:36.884Z","request_id":"req_011CeCejsGrhg9q6ept5y63r"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015HRgJ5BZDv1yuYDTRHdQHF","type":"tool_result","content":"import React, { CSSProperties } from \"react\";\nimport { Chain, type Address as AddressType } from \"viem\";\nexport type AddressProps = {\n    address?: AddressType;\n    disableAddressLink?: boolean;\n    format?: \"short\" | \"long\";\n    size?: \"xs\" | \"sm\" | \"base\" | \"lg\" | \"xl\" | \"2xl\" | \"3xl\";\n    onlyEnsOrAddress?: boolean;\n    chain?: Chain;\n    style?: CSSProperties;\n    blockExplorerAddressLink?: string;\n};\n/**\n * Address Component\n *\n * Displays an Ethereum address with ENS name resolution, avatar, and copy functionality.\n * - Resolves ENS names and displays ENS avatars when available.\n * - Shows a blockie (identicon) as fallback when no ENS avatar is available.\n * - Provides copy-to-clipboard functionality for the address.\n * - Supports linking to block explorers for address details.\n * - Displays loading skeletons while resolving ENS names.\n *\n * @param {AddressProps} props - The props for the Address component.\n * @param {AddressType} [props.address] - (Optional) The Ethereum address to display.\n * @param {boolean} [props.disableAddressLink] - (Optional) If true, disables the link to block explorer.\n * @param {\"short\" | \"long\"} [props.format] - (Optional) Display format for the address. \"short\" shows truncated version, \"long\" shows full address.\n * @param {\"xs\" | \"sm\" | \"base\" | \"lg\" | \"xl\" | \"2xl\" | \"3xl\"} [props.size=\"base\"] - (Optional) Size variant for the component display.\n * @param {boolean} [props.onlyEnsOrAddress] - (Optional) If true, shows only ENS name or address without additional UI elements.\n * @param {Chain} [props.chain] - (Optional) The blockchain network to use for ENS resolution. Defaults to mainnet.\n * @param {CSSProperties} [props.style] - (Optional) Custom CSS styles to apply to the component.\n *   Performance Warning: Always memoize style objects to prevent unnecessary re-renders.\n * @param {string} [props.blockExplorerAddressLink] - Now let me write the README."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":741,"cache_read_input_tokens":80215,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":741},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f3abb02-a418-476d-94c8-4c1845e801a6","uuid":"c8f5964f-d539-4778-ad57-25963845f1ec","timestamp":"2026-08-19T19:25:51.752Z","request_id":"req_011CeCezffXa76jMNpMoX2iz"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCezgep1r7eHaVZ4dnJV","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01G3A1iPk3WRXzxbzyzY9ZnZ","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-19T191146Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/README.md","content":"# ☕ USDC Tip Jar (Base)\n\nAn onchain tip jar that accepts **USDC tips on Base**, plus a web page with a\nlive tip feed, a form to send a tip, and a connect-wallet flow. Built on\n[Scaffold-ETH 2](https://scaffoldeth.io) (Foundry + Next.js).\n\nEverything runs **locally against a fork of Base**, so the tip jar collects the\nreal Base USDC token (`0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`) without\nspending anything or deploying to a live network.\n\n## How it works\n\n- **`packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol`** — the contract. Tippers approve\n  the jar to spend their USDC, then call `tip(amount, message)`. Every tip is\n  stored onchain and emitted as a `NewTip` event, so the frontend can render a\n  feed. The owner can `withdraw()` the collected USDC. USDC has 6 decimals, so\n  `1 USDC == 1_000_000`.\n- **`packages/nextjs`** — the web page (`app/page.tsx` + `components/tipjar/`):\n  jar stats, a tip form (handles the USDC `approve` → `tip` flow), and a live\n  feed. The connect-wallet button lives in the header.\n\nBecause the jar needs a USDC token that actually has code and balances, we run\nagainst a **Base fork** and point the frontend at the local fork\n(chain id `31337`), not at live Base.\n\n## Prerequisites\n\n- [Node](https://nodejs.org) (>= 20)\n- [Yarn](https://yarnpkg.com) v1\n- [Foundry](https://book.getfoundry.sh/getting-started/installation) (`anvil`,\n  `cast`, `forge` on your `PATH`)\n\n## Run it locally\n\nOpen **three terminals** in the project root. Run these in order.\n\n### 1. Install dependencies\n\n```bash\nyarn install\n```\n\n### 2. Start a local Base fork (terminal 1)\n\n```bash\nyarn fork\n```\n\nThis runs Anvil forking Base mainnet with chain id `31337` on\n`http://127.0.0.1:8545`. Leave it running. (It forks Base by default; set\n`FORK_URL` to a different `rpc_endpoints` alias from `foundry.toml` to fork\nsomething else.)\n\n### 3. Deploy the TipJar (terminal 2)\n\n```bash\nyarn deploy\n```\n\nThis deploys `TipJar` to the fork and writes its address + ABI into\n`packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts`. The deployer (Anvil account\n`0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720`) becomes the jar owner.\n\n### 4. Start the web app (terminal 3)\n\n```bash\nyarn start\n```\n\nOpen **http://localhost:3000**.\n\n## Try it out\n\n### Seed the feed with sample tips (optional)\n\nTo see the feed populated immediately, seed a few tips from Anvil's built-in\ntest accounts:\n\n```bash\n./scripts/seed-tips.sh\n```\n\n### Send a tip from your own wallet\n\n1. Click **Connect Wallet** in the header. On the local fork the built-in\n   **Burner Wallet** is the easiest choice.\n2. Give your connected address some USDC (and ETH for gas) on the fork:\n\n   ```bash\n   ./scripts/fund-usdc.sh <your-connected-address> 500\n   ```\n\n   (`500` = 500 USDC; the default is 1000.) This writes directly to the forked\n   USDC contract's balance storage — safe because the fork is a throwaway local\n   copy of Base.\n3. Back in the app, enter an amount and an optional message and click\n   **Send tip**. The first tip prompts a USDC `approve`, then the `tip`\n   transaction; after that the feed and stats update live.\n\n## Tests\n\nContract unit tests (run against an in-memory mock USDC, no fork needed):\n\n```bash\nyarn foundry:test\n```\n\n## Project layout\n\n```\npackages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol         # the tip jar contract\npackages/foundry/script/DeployTipJar.s.sol     # deploy script (uses Base USDC address)\npackages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol             # unit tests\npackages/nextjs/app/page.tsx                    # the tip jar page\npackages/nextjs/components/tipjar/              # JarStats, TipForm, TipFeed\npackages/nextjs/contracts/externalContracts.ts # Base USDC address + ABI (chain 31337)\nscripts/fund-usdc.sh                            # give a demo address USDC + ETH\nscripts/seed-tips.sh                            # populate the feed with sample tips\n```\n\n## Notes\n\n- **Not deployed anywhere.** The deliverable is this local project. The deploy\n  script targets the local fork. To go to real Base later, you'd run\n  `yarn deploy --network base` with a funded keystore and add Base (`8453`) to\n  `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts` and `externalContracts.ts`.\n- The Base USDC address is baked into the deploy script and the frontend's\n  external-contracts config. On the fork, that address already has code and a\n  full history, so no mock token is deployed.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":741,"cache_read_input_tokens":80215,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":741},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f3abb02-a418-476d-94c8-4c1845e801a6","uuid":"f4a80e1f-c703-4885-b79e-c77f0e4f23a6","timestamp":"2026-08-19T19:26:16.667Z","request_id":"req_011CeCezffXa76jMNpMoX2iz"}

(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f3abb02-a418-476d-94c8-4c1845e801a6","uuid":"8a604b43-4915-487f-8acd-6b7bf2ec8fb9","timestamp":"2026-08-19T19:26:16.686Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"update","filePath":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-19T191146Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/README.md","content":"# ☕ USDC Tip Jar (Base)\n\nAn onchain tip jar that accepts **USDC tips on Base**, plus a web page with a\nlive tip feed, a form to send a tip, and a connect-wallet flow. Built on\n[Scaffold-ETH 2](https://scaffoldeth.io) (Foundry + Next.js).\n\nEverything runs **locally against a fork of Base**, so the tip jar collects the\nreal Base USDC token (`0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`) without\nspending anything or deploying to a live network.\n\n## How it works\n\n- **`packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol`** — the contract. Tippers approve\n  the jar to spend their USDC, then call `tip(amount, message)`. Every tip is\n  stored onchain and emitted as a `NewTip` event, so the frontend can render a\n  feed. The owner can `withdraw()` the collected USDC. USDC has 6 decimals, so\n  `1 USDC == 1_000_000`.\n- **`packages/nextjs`** — the web page (`app/page.tsx` + `components/tipjar/`):\n  jar stats, a tip form (handles the USDC `approve` → `tip` flow), and a live\n  feed. The connect-wallet button lives in the header.\n\nBecause the jar needs a USDC token that actually has code and balances, we run\nagainst a **Base fork** and point the frontend at the local fork\n(chain id `31337`), not at live Base.\n\n## Prerequisites\n\n- [Node](https://nodejs.org) (>= 20)\n- [Yarn](https://yarnpkg.com) v1\n- [Foundry](https://book.getfoundry.sh/getting-started/installation) (`anvil`,\n  `cast`, `forge` on your `PATH`)\n\n## Run it locally\n\nOpen **three terminals** in the project root. Run these in order.\n\n### 1. Install dependencies\n\n```bash\nyarn install\n```\n\n### 2. Start a local Base fork (terminal 1)\n\n```bash\nyarn fork\n```\n\nThis runs Anvil forking Base mainnet with chain id `31337` on\n`http://127.0.0.1:8545`. Leave it running. (It forks Base by default; set\n`FORK_URL` to a different `rpc_endpoints` alias from `foundry.toml` to fork\nsomething else.)\n\n### 3. Deploy the TipJar (terminal 2)\n\n```bash\nyarn deploy\n```\n\nThis deploys `TipJar` to the fork and writes its address + ABI into\n`packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts`. The deployer (Anvil account\n`0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720`) becomes the jar owner.\n\n### 4. Start the web app (terminal 3)\n\n```bash\nyarn start\n```\n\nOpen **http://localhost:3000**.\n\n## Try it out\n\n### Seed the feed with sample tips (optional)\n\nTo see the feed populated immediately, seed a few tips from Anvil's built-in\ntest accounts:\n\n```bash\n./scripts/seed-tips.sh\n```\n\n### Send a tip from your own wallet\n\n1. Click **Connect Wallet** in the header. On the local fork the built-in\n   **Burner Wallet** is the easiest choice.\n2. Give your connected address some USDC (and ETH for gas) on the fork:\n\n   ```bash\n   ./scripts/fund-usdc.sh <your-connected-address> 500\n   ```\n\n   (`500` = 500 USDC; the default is 1000.) This writes directly to the forked\n   USDC contract's balance storage — safe because the fork is a throwaway local\n   copy of Base.\n3. Back in the app, enter an amount and an optional message and click\n   **Send tip**. The first tip prompts a USDC `approve`, then the `tip`\n   transaction; after that the feed and stats update live.\n\n## Tests\n\nContract unit tests (run against an in-memory mock USDC, no fork needed):\n\n```bash\nyarn foundry:test\n```\n\n## Project layout\n\n```\npackages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol         # the tip jar contract\npackages/foundry/script/DeployTipJar.s.sol     # deploy script (uses Base USDC address)\npackages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol             # unit tests\npackages/nextjs/app/page.tsx                    # the tip jar page\npackages/nextjs/components/tipjar/              # JarStats, TipForm, TipFeed\npackages/nextjs/contracts/externalContracts.ts # Base USDC address + ABI (chain 31337)\nscripts/fund-usdc.sh                            # give a demo address USDC + ETH\nscripts/seed-tips.sh                            # populate the feed with sample tips\n```\n\n## Notes\n\n- **Not deployed anywhere.** The deliverable is this local project. The deploy\n  script targets the local fork. To go to real Base later, you'd run\n  `yarn deploy --network base` with a funded keystore and add Base (`8453`) to\n  `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts` and `externalContracts.ts`.\n- The Base USDC address is baked into the deploy script and the frontend's\n  external-contracts config. On the fork, that address already has code and a\n  full history, so no mock token is deployed.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":83,"newStart":1,"newLines":129,"lines":["-# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2","+# ☕ USDC Tip Jar (Base)"," ","-<h4 align=\"center\">","-  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |","-  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>","-</h4>","+An onchain tip jar that accepts **USDC tips on Base**, plus a web page with a","+live tip feed, a form to send a tip, and a connect-wallet flow. On the fork, that address already has code and a","+  full history, so no mock token is deployed."]}],"originalFile":"# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n\n<h4 align=\"center\">\n  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n</h4>\n\n🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n\n⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Foundry, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n\n- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.\n- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.\n- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.\n- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.\n- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.\n\n![Debug Contracts tab](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/assets/55535804/b237af0c-5027-4849-a5c1-2e31495cccb1)\n\n## Requirements\n\nBefore you begin, you need to install the following tools:\n\n- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)\n- Yarn ([v1](https://classic.yarnpkg.com/en/docs/install/) or [v2+](https://yarnpkg.com/getting-started/install))\n- [Git](https://git-scm.com/downloads)\n\n## Quickstart\n\nTo get started with Scaffold-ETH 2, follow the steps below:\n\n1. Install dependencies if it was skipped in CLI:\n\n```\ncd my-dapp-example\nyarn install\n```\n\n2. Run a local network in the first terminal:\n\n```\nyarn chain\n```\n\nThis command starts a local Ethereum network using Foundry. The network runs on your local machine and can be used for testing and development. You can customize the network configuration in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`.\n\n3. On a second terminal, deploy the test contract:\n\n```\nyarn deploy\n```\n\nThis command deploys a test smart contract to the local network. The contract is located in `packages/foundry/contracts` and can be modified to suit your needs. The `yarn deploy` command uses the deploy script located in `packages/foundry/script` to deploy the contract to the network. You can also customize the deploy script.\n\n4. On a third terminal, start your NextJS app:\n\n```\nyarn start\n```\n\nVisit your app on: `http://localhost:3000`. You can interact with your smart contract using the `Debug Contracts` page. You can tweak the app config in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.\n\nRun smart contract test with `yarn foundry:test`\n\n- Edit your smart contracts in `packages/foundry/contracts`\n- Edit your frontend homepage at `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`. For guidance on [routing](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/defining-routes) and configuring [pages/layouts](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/pages-and-layouts) checkout the Next.js documentation.\n- Edit your deployment scripts in `packages/foundry/script`\n\n\n## Documentation\n\nVisit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.\n\nTo know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).\n\n## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2\n\nWe welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!\n\nPlease see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2.","userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCfCzGJSZ8FE4m3cFrhy","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Everything's built, running locally, and verified. Here's the summary.\n\n## USDC Tip Jar for Base — done and running locally\n\n**What was built** (Scaffold-ETH 2, Foundry + Next.js) in `usdc-tip-jar/`:\n\n- **Contract** — `packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol`. Tippers `approve` the jar, then call `tip(amount, message)`; every tip is stored onchain and emitted as `NewTip` (powers the feed). Owner can `withdraw()`. Collects the real Base USDC (`0x8335…2913`). Unit tests in `TipJar.t.sol` pass (feed/totals, zero-tip revert, owner-only withdraw).\n- **Frontend** — `app/page.tsx` + `components/tipjar/`: `JarStats` (total tipped / count / jar balance), `TipForm` (handles the USDC approve→tip flow, balance check, presets, 280-char message), `TipFeed` (live, newest-first). Connect-wallet button is in the header (RainbowKit + burner wallet for local).\n\n**Why a fork:** the jar needs a USDC token that actually has code and balances, so it runs against a **Base fork** (`yarn fork`, chain id 31337), with the frontend pointed at the local fork per the playbook — not live Base. Two helper scripts: `scripts/fund-usdc.sh` (give any address USDC+ETH) and `scripts/seed-tips.sh` (populate the feed).\n\n**Verified end-to-end:**\n- Fork has real Base USDC (`symbol()` → \"USDC\", code present), interval mining on.\n- Contract deployed to the fork; ABI/address exported to the frontend.\n- The exact approve→tip sequence works on-chain (seed script) — feed now shows 3 tips, $17.5 total.\n- Headless-Chrome render confirms the client reads live fork data (stats + all three tip messages) with no runtime errors; type-check clean, lint 0 errors.\n\n**Currently running:** Base fork on `127.0.0.1:8545` (200), app on **http://localhost:3000** (200), TipJar holding 17.5 USDC across 3 tips.\n\n**README.md** rewritten with the full local setup: prerequisites, `yarn install` → `yarn fork` → `yarn deploy` → `yarn start`, plus how to fund your wallet and send a tip. Nothing was deployed to any live network, as requested.\n\nOne note: `yarn fork` now defaults to Base (I changed the scaffold's fork script from its awkward `mainnet` positional default to `FORK_URL:-base`), so a fresh `yarn fork` forks Base with no extra flags."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1452,"cache_read_input_tokens":89806,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1452},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f3abb02-a418-476d-94c8-4c1845e801a6","uuid":"5c87bdd9-de41-40db-ae02-dc536344d637","timestamp":"2026-08-19T19:28:45.240Z","request_id":"req_011CeCfCy6rNzTNfXUyoY4J9"}
